<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">// Add documents with sparse embeddings (BM25)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">})</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">// Query with sparse vector</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> sparseRank</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> =</span><span style="color:#6F42C1"> Knn</span><span style="color:#24292E">({ query: </span><span style="color:#032F62">"ML"</span><span style="color:#24292E">, key: </span><span style="color:#032F62">"sparse_embedding"</span><span style="color:#24292E"> });</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A737D">// Build and execute search</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> search</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> new</span><span style="color:#6F42C1"> Search</span><span style="color:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">  .</span><span style="color:#6F42C1">rank</span><span style="color:#24292E">(sparseRank)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">  .</span><span style="color:#6F42C1">limit</span><span style="color:#24292E">(</span><span style="color:#005CC5">10</span><span style="color:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E">  .</span><span style="color:#6F42C1">select</span><span style="color:#24292E">(</span><span style="color:#005CC5">K</span><span style="color:#24292E">.</span><span style="color:#005CC5">DOCUMENT</span><span style="color:#24292E">, </span><span style="color:#005CC5">K</span><span style="color:#24292E">.</span><span style="color:#005CC5">SCORE</span><span style="color:#24292E">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49">const</span><span style="color:#005CC5"> results</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> =</span><span style="color:#D73A49"> await</span><span style="color:#24292E"> collection.</span><span style="color:#6F42C1">search</span><span style="color:#24292E">(search);</span></span></code></pre></div></div><div class="h-1 bg-gray-300 border-y border-chroma-black cursor-ns-resize hover:bg-gray-400 transition-colors shrink-0"></div><div class="flex flex-col border-l border-r border-b border-chroma-black bg-[#1e1e1e] overflow-hidden" style="height:40%"><div class="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2d2d2d] text-[#cccccc] text-xs border-b border-[#3e3e3e] shrink-0">Terminal Output</div><div class="p-4 text-xs text-[#cccccc] font-mono overflow-auto flex-1"><pre class="whitespace-pre-wrap">$ node sparse-search.js
